# E2-T05 · Plans and prices: Plan, Price, PriceResolver, EntryFeeCalculator, public plans (S05 WP-05-C)

## Objective
Work package WP-05-C: the club's offer (monthly plans, packs, single class) with dated prices, ready for signup (E3) and billing (E8).

## Spec references
S05 §3 (`Plan {type MONTHLY|PACK|SINGLE_CLASS, name, shortText/longText LocalizedText, dogsIncluded, sessions?, validityMonths?, requiredModules[], order, active}`, `Price {planId, concept, amount Money, taxPercent, validFrom, validTo, status SCHEDULED|CURRENT|SUPERSEDED}`), R-05-16 (create supersedes), R-05-17 (`PRICE_LOCKED` once used), R-05-18 (`entryFeeAmount` from `billing.entryFeePerDog`), module gating (`PACKS`, `SINGLE_CLASS`, `BILLING`) · tests **T-05-02, 03, 04, 07, 17, 18** · `docs/DECISIONS_PENDENTS.md` B2/B3 (cash monthly, no VAT at the Cànic: `taxPercent = 0`).

## Context to load
S05 §3, §4, §6 · CATALEG_PARAMETRES `billing.*` · CATALEG_MODULS · this file.

## Steps
0. **Spec change of 08-09 (organizer, B10)**: `Plan.billingMode` (`MONTHLY_FEE · MAINTENANCE`, only when `type = MONTHLY`; the Cànic «Teràpia» seed = `MAINTENANCE`, S05 §12) is a plan attribute — it replaces the former `Member.billingMode` (E2-T06 does not expose one). `PublicPlan`/`Plan` schemas carry it; D8 shows it as a select on the plan form (E2-W04). Snapshot regenerated.
1. Entities + endpoints of S05 §6 (`/plans*`, `/prices*`, `/public/{clubSlug}/plans` without tenant header: club by slug, only active plans with current prices and texts in the requested locale).
2. `PriceResolver.current(planId, concept, date)`, `EntryFeeCalculator` (per dog, family discount proposal `billing.familyDiscountPercentFromSecondDog` only as a suggestion), `taxPercent` on every price (0 for the Cànic seed).
3. Module branches: creating a `PACK` plan with `PACKS` off → `MODULE_DISABLED`; `/prices` with `BILLING` off → `MODULE_DISABLED`.
4. Audit `PLAN_*`, `PRICE_*`.

## Verification (run all; paste full output in the report)
- `./mvnw -q verify` → green with T-05-02, 03, 04, 07, 17, 18.

## Definition of Done
Plans/prices complete; public endpoint; module gating; audit.

## Evidence to return
`verify` output; `curl /public/canic/plans` JSON from the seed (E2-T11) or a test fixture.

---

## Executor report
Completed E2-T05 on 2026-09-09. Full verification passes 244 unit/contract tests and 365 integration tests, with no failures, errors or skips. The public fixture curl and OpenAPI snapshot checks pass. E2-T03 is verified; no awaiting-verification dependency was required. E2-T04's existing team concurrency test exposed the shared immediate-retry exhaustion described below; no E2-T04 task file was edited.

### Branch / commits
Working-tree edits only. No git write command, branch or commit was created. The session publish script owns publication.

### Evidence (full command outputs)
Maven uses the existing writable temporary wrapper/dependency cache because the sandbox cannot write the default user cache. Commands below ran with:

```sh
export MAVEN_USER_HOME=/private/tmp/e1-t09-maven
export MAVEN_OPTS=-Dmaven.repo.local=/private/tmp/e1-t09-maven/repository
```

Full stdout/stderr follows, with tokens/hashes truncated under AGENTS.md. No output lines are omitted. The initial focused failure was the Mongo date-range resolver; current resolution now uses the stored typed intervals and PriceRules' inclusive club-date calculation. The first full verify exposed five immediate retries exhausting before a concurrent team transaction committed. IdentityTransactions now waits 25/50/100/200 ms between its existing five attempts and preserves interruption; new T-05-20 unit tests cover transient retries, business errors and cancellation. The final verify below includes the fix and the existing team regression.

Command: `./mvnw -q -DskipTests compile` · exit 0.

No stdout/stderr.

Command: `./mvnw -q -Dtest=OfferCalculatorsTest,PlansIT,E2ContractIT test` · exit 1.

### Rules and tests implemented (R-xx-nn / T-xx-nn)
- R-05-02/04/06/20, T-05-06/08/17/19/20: plan CRUD, complete ordering, code uniqueness, optimistic versions, active/inactive projections, usage/type/deletion guards and unchanged existing member assignments.
- R-05-15/16/17, T-05-02/03/20/30: inclusive dated intervals, atomic CLOSED/CREATED supersession, immutable started/invoiced terms, last-billed-period guards, scheduled correction/deletion, explicit null reopening, currency/tax validation, calculated status/periodicity, local midnight and concurrent writers. Invoice documents remain unchanged; rollback leaves no partial prices, audit or outbox.
- B10, R-05-18/18b/19, T-05-04/07: Plan.billingMode, entry-fee modes, shared Money rounding, family-tariff suggestions, parameter-driven pack-to-monthly proposals, and price lines in ca/es/en for MONTHLY/PACK/SINGLE_CLASS/no current price.
- R-05-21 and module branches, T-05-17/18/19: slug/key public access without tenant headers, locale intersection/fallback and translation maps, active/showOnWeb/type-module visibility, reduced authenticated readers, disabled BILLING omission, cache/Vary headers, same-key cross-club rejection, every plan/price endpoint's roles/tenant checks and valid impersonation denial on ADMIN endpoints. The existing public-route per-IP limiter remains in force.
- S14 R-14-09, T-14-12: @Audited CATALOG_CHANGED and approved PlanChanged/PriceChanged outbox events in the aggregate transaction; closed-catalog and API-response fixtures updated. Snapshot regeneration and complete architecture/coverage verification pass.

### Assumptions
- This task's explicit B10 change and current S05 §3 govern the older model annex's Member.billingMode row. MONTHLY requests may omit the new field for compatibility and default to MONTHLY_FEE; other plan types reject a supplied billingMode and omit it at read time. No census mutation contract was changed.
- S05 §3's calculated EXPIRED status governs the task's illustrative SUPERSEDED status. Price dates persist as ISO business-date strings; all today/month boundaries use injected ClubClock. Price.periodicity is derived from its concept. A locked price accepts numerically identical existing values but rejects changes except a permitted validTo edit; null reopens an interval only without overlap.
- Catalogs win on HTTP/audit names: INVALID_API_KEY=403, CURRENCY_MISMATCH=422, PLAN_IN_USE/PRICE_LOCKED/PRICE_OVERLAP=409. Creating/changing a disabled type follows S05's 400 VALIDATION_ERROR with MODULE_DISABLED at field type; /prices remains 404 MODULE_DISABLED. All Plan/Price audit actions use approved CATALOG_CHANGED, not invented PLAN_*/PRICE_* aliases.
- OfferUsage is a read adapter for census/billing collections, not an implementation of those verticals. It checks members, pack_balances, invoice_lines, invoices.lines and collections by planId/priceId. Invoice-period input is periodTo or period (YYYY-MM, ISO date, {to}, or BSON business date); unknown referenced periods block chronological changes with PRICE_LOCKED. Every read binds clubId; deletion/type checks include historical references. Owning verticals must align final schemas and participate in the Plan catalog lock before writing references.
- An unused plan can change type while retaining price history. Only compatible concepts appear in its current offered prices; old prices retain lock protection. Deleting an unused plan removes its unreferenced price children atomically. No invoice/member/pack-consumption data is changed.
- Entry-fee rounding reuses Money's HALF_EVEN minor-unit calculation. Family discount output is a proposed tariff total, never an automatic price mutation. PlanChange accepts previous/new plans, last-pack session count and an alreadyApplied flag; E3/E8/S13 must atomically record one-use pack eligibility when accepting the proposal.
- Public website keys use the model's Club.publicApiKeyHash as lowercase SHA-256 hex, with constant-time digest comparison. Club-as-code preserves it. Key provisioning/rotation is outside this task; the actual curl uses a generated runtime-only fictional fixture key. No operational key or E2-T11 seed catalog was created. Public plans intentionally ignore showOnSignup and honor active/showOnWeb; caching varies on X-Api-Key and Accept-Language.
- The shared IdentityTransactions helper is already used by catalog team services. Its bounded backoff fixes the observed full-suite race and protects this task's plan/price operations without adding business parameters or changing the existing retry count.

### Questions / catalog proposals
None blocking; no parameter, event, notification or error catalog additions. Two entries in roadmap/MESSAGES.md ask the organizer to align stale task/spec examples and carry the explicit census/billing/key-provisioning handoffs into their owning verticals.
